[31m+ function scheduleIdlePreload(cb: () => void, timeout = 1000) {[39m
[31m+   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;[39m
[31m+   const ric = (window as unknown as { requestIdleCallback?: (cb: () => void, opts?: { timeout: number }) => number }).requestIdleCallback;[39m
[31m+   if (typeof ric === "function") {[39m
[31m+     ric(cb, { timeout });[39m
[31m+   } else {[39m
[31m+     setTimeout(cb, timeout);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ function useHeroStageScale() {[39m
[31m+   const [scale, setScale] = useState(() => heroStageScale(window.innerWidth));[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const onResize = () => setScale(heroStageScale(window.innerWidth));[39m
[31m+     window.addEventListener("resize", onResize);[39m
[31m+     return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", onResize);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+   return scale;[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+ const PROMO_BANNER_ACTIVE_MARGIN_PX = 600;[39m
[31m+ /** "40% off + Buy 1 Get 3" for the one-line phone banner (drops "Limited Time:" and "Instantly"). */[39m
[31m+ const PROMO_DEAL_SHORT = PROMO_DEAL_LINE.replace(/^limited time:\s*/i, "").replace(/\s*instantly\s*$/i, "");[39m
[31m+ const PROMO_BANNER_GRADIENT =[39m
[31m+   "linear-gradient(90deg, #172554 0%, #1d4ed8 28%, #2563eb 50%, #1d4ed8 72%, #172554 100%)";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function copyTextToClipboard(text: string): Promise<boolean> {[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     if (navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {[39m
[31m+       await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);[39m
[31m+       return true;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   } catch {[39m
[31m+     // Fall through to the legacy path below.[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const input = document.createElement("textarea");[39m
[31m+     input.value = text;[39m
[31m+     input.setAttribute("readonly", "");[39m
[31m+     input.style.position = "fixed";[39m
[31m+     input.style.left = "-9999px";[39m
[31m+     input.style.top = "0";[39m
[31m+     document.body.appendChild(input);[39m
[31m+     input.focus();[39m
[31m+     input.select();[39m
[31m+     const ok = document.execCommand("copy");[39m
[31m+     document.body.removeChild(input);[39m
[31m+     return ok;[39m
[31m+   } catch {[39m
[31m+     return false;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function PromoCountdownPill({ compact = false, pulse }: { compact?: boolean; pulse: boolean }) {[39m
[31m+   const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now());[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const id = window.setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);[39m
[31m+     return () => window.clearInterval(id);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const left = formatHoursLeft(PROMO_DEADLINE, now);[39m
[31m+   if (left.expired) return null;[39m
[31m+   const totalHours = Number(left.hh);[39m
[31m+   const days = Math.floor(totalHours / 24);[39m
[31m+   const hh = String(totalHours % 24).padStart(2, "0");[39m
[31m+   const label = compact[39m
[31m+     ? `${days > 0 ? `${days}D ` : ""}${hh}:${left.mm}:${left.ss} Left`[39m
[31m+     : `${days > 0 ? `${days}D ` : ""}${hh}H ${left.mm}M ${left.ss}S Left`;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <span[39m
[31m+       role="timer"[39m
[31m+       aria-label={`${days > 0 ? `${days} days ` : ""}${Number(hh)} hours ${Number(left.mm)} minutes left`}[39m
[31m+       className={`inline-flex shrink-0 items-center rounded-full font-['Inter:Bold',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none tracking-[0.04em] tabular-nums text-white ${compact ? "h-[16px] px-[7px] text-[7.5px]" : "h-[24px] px-[12px] text-[11px] lg:h-[25px] lg:text-[12px]"}`}[39m
[31m+       style={{[39m
[31m+         background: "#DC2626",[39m
[31m+         border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.28)",[39m
[31m+         boxShadow: "0 2px 8px rgba(220,38,38,0.35), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.2)",[39m
[31m+         animation: pulse ? "fyt-promo-urgency-pulse 2.4s ease-in-out infinite" : undefined,[39m
[31m+       }}[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       {label}[39m
[31m+     </span>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function PromoBanner() {[39m
[31m+   const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();[39m
[31m+   const { compact } = useContext(HeaderModeContext);[39m
[31m+   const bannerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [nearViewport, setNearViewport] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const copiedTimerRef = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const el = bannerRef.current;[39m
[31m+     if (!el || typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") return;[39m
[31m+     const observer = new IntersectionObserver([39m
[31m+       (entries) => setNearViewport(!!entries[0]?.isIntersecting),[39m
[31m+       { rootMargin: `${PROMO_BANNER_ACTIVE_MARGIN_PX}px 0px ${PROMO_BANNER_ACTIVE_MARGIN_PX}px 0px` },[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     observer.observe(el);[39m
[31m+     return () => observer.disconnect();[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => () => {[39m
[31m+     if (copiedTimerRef.current !== undefined) window.clearTimeout(copiedTimerRef.current);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   function scrollToPricing() {[39m
[31m+     window.location.hash = "challenge";[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   function stopBannerNav(e: MouseEvent) {[39m
[31m+     e.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+     e.stopPropagation();[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   async function copyCode(e: MouseEvent) {[39m
[31m+     stopBannerNav(e);[39m
[31m+     const ok = await copyTextToClipboard(PROMO_CODE);[39m
[31m+     if (!ok) return;[39m
[31m+     setCopied(true);[39m
[31m+     if (copiedTimerRef.current !== undefined) window.clearTimeout(copiedTimerRef.current);[39m
[31m+     copiedTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1600);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   function CodePill({ compact = false }: { compact?: boolean }) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <button[39m
[31m+         type="button"[39m
[31m+         onMouseDown={stopBannerNav}[39m
[31m+         onClick={copyCode}[39m
[31m+         className={`inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-[5px] rounded-full border-0 px-[10px] cursor-pointer transition-transform duration-200 hover:scale-[1.04] active:scale-[0.97] ${compact ? "h-[18px]" : "h-[22px]"}`}[39m
[31m+         style={{ background: copied ? "#14532d" : "#0B1220", boxShadow: "0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.28), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.12)" }}[39m
[31m+         aria-label={copied ? `Copied ${PROMO_CODE}` : `Copy promo code ${PROMO_CODE}`}[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <span className={`font-['Inter:Bold',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none tracking-[0.06em] text-[#93c5fd] ${compact ? "text-[8px]" : "text-[9px]"}`}>[39m
[31m+           {copied ? "Copied" : "Code"}[39m
[31m+         </span>[39m
[31m+         <span className={`font-['Inter:Bold',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none tracking-[0.04em] text-white ${compact ? "text-[9px]" : "text-[10px]"}`}>{PROMO_CODE}</span>[39m
[31m+       </button>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div[39m
[31m+       ref={bannerRef}[39m
[31m+       role="link"[39m
[31m+       tabIndex={0}[39m
[31m+       onClick={scrollToPricing}[39m
[31m+       onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") { e.preventDefault(); scrollToPricing(); } }}[39m
[31m+       aria-label="View current promotions, jump to pricing"[39m
[31m+       className="group/promo relative z-[2] shrink-0 w-full cursor-pointer overflow-hidden"[39m
[31m+       style={{[39m
[31m+         background: PROMO_BANNER_GRADIENT,[39m
[31m+         boxShadow: "inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.22), inset 0 -1px 0 rgba(0,0,0,0.25), 0 8px 28px rgba(37,99,235,0.35)",[39m
[31m+         contain: "layout",[39m
[31m+       }}[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+         className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-px"[39m
[31m+         style={{ background: "linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(255,255,255,0.55), transparent)" }}[39m
[31m+       />[39m
[31m+       {!reduceMotion && ([39m
[31m+         <div[39m
[31m+           aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+           className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 w-1/3 opacity-40"[39m
[31m+           style={{[39m
[31m+             background: "linear-gradient(100deg, transparent 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0.18) 45%, rgba(255,255,255,0.28) 50%, rgba(255,255,255,0.18) 55%, transparent 100%)",[39m
[31m+             animation: nearViewport ? "fyt-promo-sheen 4.5s ease-in-out infinite" : undefined,[39m
[31m+           }}[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Mobile. At the top: 2 lines (deal + CODE, then benefits + countdown).[39m
[31m+           After scrolling: folds to one slim line (short deal + CODE + countdown). */}[39m
[31m+       <div className={`relative mx-auto flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center px-[6px] transition-[padding] duration-300 ease-out md:hidden ${compact ? "py-[6px]" : "py-[7px]"}`}>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-x-[6px] whitespace-nowrap">[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none text-white whitespace-nowrap text-[10px] tracking-[0.01em] min-[380px]:text-[10.5px]">[39m
[31m+             {compact ? PROMO_DEAL_SHORT : PROMO_DEAL_LINE}[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+           <CodePill compact />[39m
[31m+           {compact && <PromoCountdownPill compact pulse={!reduceMotion && nearViewport} />}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div[39m
[31m+           className="grid w-full transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity] duration-300 ease-out"[39m
[31m+           style={{ gridTemplateRows: compact ? "0fr" : "1fr", opacity: compact ? 0 : 1 }}[39m
[31m+           aria-hidden={compact || undefined}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <div className="overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-x-[5px] whitespace-nowrap pt-[5px]">[39m
[31m+               {PROMO_BENEFITS.map((text, i) => ([39m
[31m+                 <Fragment key={text}>[39m
[31m+                   {i > 0 && <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-[8px] w-px shrink-0 bg-white/35" />}[39m
[31m+                   <span className="font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none text-white whitespace-nowrap text-[8.5px] tracking-[0.01em] text-white/85">[39m
[31m+                     {text}[39m
[31m+                   </span>[39m
[31m+                 </Fragment>[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+               {!compact && <PromoCountdownPill compact pulse={!reduceMotion && nearViewport} />}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Desktop / tablet: deal first, then benefits, then CODE + countdown.[39m
[31m+           Each group stays on one line, so a narrow screen wraps between groups, never mid-offer. */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="relative mx-auto hidden min-h-[36px] w-full max-w-[1440px] flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-x-[12px] gap-y-[6px] px-[12px] py-[8px] md:flex lg:gap-x-[14px] lg:px-[20px] xl:px-[24px]">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-x-[10px] whitespace-nowrap lg:gap-x-[14px]">[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none text-white whitespace-nowrap text-[12px] tracking-[0.02em] lg:text-[13px] xl:text-[14px]">[39m
[31m+             {PROMO_DEAL_LINE}[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+           {PROMO_BENEFITS.map((text) => ([39m
[31m+             <Fragment key={text}>[39m
[31m+               <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-[12px] w-px shrink-0 bg-white/25" />[39m
[31m+               <span className="font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none text-white whitespace-nowrap text-[11px] tracking-[0.02em] text-white/85 lg:text-[12px] xl:text-[13px]">[39m
[31m+                 {text}[39m
[31m+               </span>[39m
[31m+             </Fragment>[39m
[31m+           ))}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-x-[8px] whitespace-nowrap lg:gap-x-[10px]">[39m
[31m+           <CodePill />[39m
[31m+           <PromoCountdownPill pulse={!reduceMotion && nearViewport} />[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // The radar sweep's implementation was rebuilt below to be iOS-safe (single[39m
[31m+ // element, no blur filter, ~1/4 the layer area of the original — see the[39m
[31m+ // comment on the sweep-arm block). Flip to false again only if you need to[39m
[31m+ // rule it out during future debugging; the animation/timing logic itself[39m
[31m+ // (HERO_SWEEP_CSS, per-label glow keyframes) is unchanged from before.[39m
[31m+ const HERO_RADAR_ENABLED = true;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function HeroStage({[39m
[31m+   labelScale = 1,[39m
[31m+   mobileLabels = false,[39m
[31m+   beamSuffix = "",[39m
[31m+   beamReachScale = 1,[39m
[31m+   sweepPaused = false,[39m
[31m+ }: {[39m
[31m+   labelScale?: number;[39m
[31m+   mobileLabels?: boolean;[39m
[31m+   beamSuffix?: string;[39m
[31m+   beamReachScale?: number;[39m
[31m+   sweepPaused?: boolean;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <>[39m
[31m+       {/* Orbit rings — top halves of concentric ellipses */}[39m
[31m+       <svg className="absolute inset-0" width="1440" height="1080" viewBox="0 0 1440 1080" fill="none">[39m
[31m+         {HERO_ORBIT_RINGS.map((r, i) => ([39m
[31m+           <ellipse key={i} cx={r.cx} cy={r.cy} rx={r.rx} ry={r.ry} stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.07)" strokeWidth="1" />[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+         {mobileLabels &&[39m
[31m+           MOBILE_ORBIT_LABELS.map(({ label, left, top }) => ([39m
[31m+             <line key={label} x1={720} y1={720} x2={left} y2={top + 5} stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.12)" strokeWidth="1" />[39m
[31m+           ))}[39m
[31m+       </svg>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Core glow behind the node */}[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         className="absolute"[39m
[31m+         style={{[39m
[31m+           width: 400, height: 340, left: 520, top: 550,[39m
[31m+           background: "radial-gradient(50% 50% at 50% 50%, rgba(115,166,255,0.32) 0%, rgba(59,130,245,0.13) 42%, rgba(10,36,92,0) 76%)",[39m
[31m+           filter: "blur(30px)",[39m
[31m+         }}[39m
[31m+       />[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {(mobileLabels ? MOBILE_ORBIT_LABELS : HERO_ORBIT_LABELS).map(({ label, left, top }, i) => {[39m
[31m+         const layout = mobileLabels ? MOBILE_LABEL_LAYOUT[i] : null;[39m
[31m+         const { items, textAlign, translateX, transformOrigin } = mobileLabelBoxCss(layout?.anchor);[39m
[31m+         const textSizeClass = layout ? "text-[26px] leading-[34px]" : "text-[22px] leading-[29px] whitespace-nowrap";[39m
[31m+         const glowClass = `hero-glow${beamSuffix}-${i}`;[39m
[31m+         return ([39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             key={label}[39m
[31m+             className={`absolute flex flex-col ${items} gap-[12px]`}[39m
[31m+             style={{[39m
[31m+               left,[39m
[31m+               top,[39m
[31m+               width: layout?.wrapWidth,[39m
[31m+               transform: `translateX(${translateX}) scale(${labelScale})`,[39m
[31m+               transformOrigin,[39m
[31m+             }}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <div className="rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ width: 10, height: 10, background: "#3B82F6", boxShadow: "0px 0px 8px rgba(59,130,246,0.8)" }} />[39m
[31m+             <p[39m
[31m+               className={`font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-medium ${textSizeClass} tracking-[-0.44px] text-white/90`}[39m
[31m+               style={{ textAlign }}[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {label}[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+             <div className={`${glowClass} absolute inset-0 flex flex-col ${items} gap-[12px]`} style={{ opacity: 0, animationPlayState: sweepPaused ? "paused" : "running", display: HERO_RADAR_ENABLED ? undefined : "none" }} aria-hidden>[39m
[31m+               <div className="rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ width: 10, height: 10, background: "#ffffff", boxShadow: "0px 0px 16px 4px rgba(255,255,255,0.9)" }} />[39m
[31m+               <p[39m
[31m+                 className={`font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-medium ${textSizeClass} tracking-[-0.44px] text-white`}[39m
[31m+                 style={{ textShadow: "0px 0px 16px rgba(255,255,255,0.85)", textAlign }}[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {label}[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       })}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {HERO_RADAR_ENABLED && ([39m
[31m+         // Lightweight rebuild of the radar sweep. The original heavy[39m
[31m+         // version stacked three separately blurred (filter: blur(6px)) and[39m
[31m+         // masked panels inside an oversized 1720x1720px layer — that[39m
[31m+         // combination (large size + blur filter + mask-image + animation,[39m
[31m+         // times three) is a known iOS Safari GPU-crash pattern. A follow-up[39m
[31m+         // attempt using conic-gradient + mask-image avoided the crash but[39m
[31m+         // rendered with hard, aliased edges on-device instead of a soft[39m
[31m+         // fade — mask-image doesn't anti-alias reliably in combination with[39m
[31m+         // conic-gradient across browsers. This version drops mask-image[39m
[31m+         // entirely: a single radial-gradient, which has smooth built-in[39m
[31m+         // falloff on its own, shaped into a narrow cone that visually[39m
[31m+         // originates from the exact center pivot.[39m
[31m+         <div[39m
[31m+           className={`absolute hero-sweep-arm${beamSuffix}`}[39m
[31m+           style={{[39m
[31m+             width: 900 * beamReachScale, height: 900 * beamReachScale,[39m
[31m+             left: 720 - (450 * beamReachScale), top: 720 - (450 * beamReachScale),[39m
[31m+             transformOrigin: "50% 50%",[39m
[31m+             animationPlayState: sweepPaused ? "paused" : "running",[39m
[31m+           }}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           {/* Rotate:0 baseline points DOWN — "Rewards on Demand" (rotate: 0[39m
[31m+               in heroOrbit.ts) sits below the node, not above it — so this[39m
[31m+               cone's tip sits at the wrapper's center (50%,50%) and it[39m
[31m+               extends toward the bottom, matching that baseline. */}[39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             className="absolute"[39m
[31m+             style={{[39m
[31m+               left: "50%", top: "50%",[39m
[31m+               width: 220 * beamReachScale, height: 400 * beamReachScale,[39m
[31m+               transform: "translateX(-50%)",[39m
[31m+               background:[39m
[31m+                 "radial-gradient(ellipse 50% 100% at 50% 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0.92) 0%, rgba(207,226,255,0.62) 16%, rgba(124,176,255,0.36) 40%, rgba(59,130,246,0.16) 65%, transparent 85%)",[39m
[31m+             }}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Node ring + core node with FYT logo (spec: 134px ring at 653,653; 96px node at 672,672) */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="absolute rounded-full border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.09)]" style={{ width: 134, height: 134, left: 653, top: 653 }} />[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         className="absolute rounded-full border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.15)] flex items-center justify-center"[39m
[31m+         style={{[39m
[31m+           width: 96, height: 96, left: 672, top: 672,[39m
[31m+           background: "linear-gradient(180deg, #1A1F2A 0%, #080A10 100%)",[39m
[31m+           boxShadow: "0px 0px 30px rgba(59,130,246,0.42), inset 0px 1px 0px rgba(255,255,255,0.13)",[39m
[31m+         }}[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <img alt="" src={imgImg10782} className="w-[70px] h-[66px] object-contain" />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function HeroStageMobile({ sweepPaused = false }: { sweepPaused?: boolean }) {[39m

[31m+       }}[39m

[31m+       <HeroStage labelScale={heroLabelScale(scale)} mobileLabels beamSuffix="-mobile" beamReachScale={1.55} sweepPaused={sweepPaused} />[39m

[31m+ function TrustStripDesktop() {[39m
[31m+   return ([39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+ // Award badge — sits to the right of the trust strip on desktop[39m
[31m+ // (TrustStripDesktop, equal-width columns so both sides balance) and in[39m
[31m+ // its own bar directly below the hero on mobile (AwardBadgeMobileBar).[39m
[31m+ // Styled to match the trust pill's own understated chrome (subtle[39m
[31m+ // border + blur) rather than standing out with a glow.[39m
[31m+ function AwardBadge() {[39m
[31m+   // Image and text have separate behavior: hovering/tapping the badge[39m
[31m+   // IMAGE zooms it in place (magnifying-glass feel), never navigates.[39m
[31m+   // Tapping/clicking the TEXT navigates to the award page. `zoomed` is[39m
[31m+   // the tap-to-toggle state for touch devices, which have no hover;[39m
[31m+   // `whileHover` covers the mouse case on desktop on top of it.[39m
[31m+   const [zoomed, setZoomed] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [hovered, setHovered] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Reset the zoom automatically on any page scroll (desktop hover state[39m
[31m+   // and mobile tap-toggle state alike), since a hover/tap can be left[39m
[31m+   // "stuck" zoomed in while the user then scrolls the page away.[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!zoomed && !hovered) return;[39m
[31m+     const handleScroll = () => {[39m
[31m+       setZoomed(false);[39m
[31m+       setHovered(false);[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     window.addEventListener("scroll", handleScroll, { passive: true });[39m
[31m+     return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", handleScroll);[39m
[31m+   }, [zoomed, hovered]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const isZoomed = zoomed || hovered;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <motion.div[39m
[31m+       initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 14 }}[39m
[31m+       whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}[39m

[31m+         background: "rgba(212,175,127,0.06)",[39m
[31m+         borderColor: "rgba(212,175,127,0.3)",[39m
[31m+       }}[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       <motion.button[39m
[31m+         type="button"[39m

[31m+ function HeroBackground() {[39m
[31m+   // The radar sweep below (.hero-sweep-arm) is a pure CSS animation with[39m
[31m+   // `infinite alternate` — nothing was ever pausing it, so unlike every[39m
[31m+   // other heavy visual on this page (globe, hero WebGL scene, promo[39m
[31m+   // marquee) it kept rotating/repainting for the entire session even[39m
[31m+   // minutes after you'd scrolled far away from the hero. A large layer with[39m
[31m+   // blur + mask-image that never stops costs real, permanent GPU/compositor[39m
[31m+   // overhead that just accumulates as you keep scrolling through the rest[39m
[31m+   // of the page. Pause it via IntersectionObserver like the marquee already[39m
[31m+   // does.[39m
[31m+   const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [nearViewport, setNearViewport] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const el = containerRef.current;[39m
[31m+     if (!el || typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") return;[39m
[31m+     const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => setNearViewport(!!entries[0]?.isIntersecting), {[39m
[31m+       rootMargin: "200px 0px 200px 0px",[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     observer.observe(el);[39m
[31m+     return () => observer.disconnect();[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div[39m
[31m+       ref={containerRef}[39m
[31m+       className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden pointer-events-none"[39m
[31m+       style={{ background: "linear-gradient(180deg, #03060d 0%, #050913 100%)" }}[39m
[31m+       aria-hidden[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       {HERO_RADAR_ENABLED && <style>{HERO_SWEEP_CSS}</style>}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Same backdrop as the dark redesign sections: navy base, faint[39m
[31m+           technical grid, two soft blue glows (no star field). All static (no animation), so it costs nothing per frame. */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="absolute inset-0 fyt-grid-dark" />[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         className="absolute rounded-full"[39m
[31m+         style={{ left: "-12%", top: "-10%", width: 720, height: 720, background: "radial-gradient(circle, rgba(37,99,235,0.2), transparent 65%)" }}[39m
[31m+       />[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         className="absolute rounded-full"[39m
[31m+         style={{ right: "-10%", bottom: "-6%", width: 640, height: 640, background: "radial-gradient(circle, rgba(59,130,246,0.14), transparent 65%)" }}[39m
[31m+       />[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Fixed 1440×1080 stage centered horizontally; scene coords are spec px */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-0 hidden lg:block" style={{ width: 1440, height: 1080, transform: "translateX(-50%)" }}>[39m
[31m+         <HeroStage sweepPaused={!nearViewport} />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       <HeroStageMobile sweepPaused={!nearViewport} />[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Vignette (spec: radial at 50% 62%) */}[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         className="absolute inset-0"[39m
[31m+         style={{ background: "radial-gradient(82% 82% at 50% 62%, rgba(3,6,13,0) 40%, rgba(3,6,13,0.35) 78%, rgba(3,6,13,0.75) 100%)" }}[39m
[31m+       />[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const HERO_WORD_REVEAL_CSS = `[39m
[31m+ @keyframes hero-word-in {[39m
[31m+   from { transform: translateY(115%); opacity: 0; }[39m
[31m+   to { transform: translateY(0%); opacity: 1; }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ .hero-word-in {[39m
[31m+   display: inline-block;[39m
[31m+   animation: hero-word-in 0.65s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) both;[39m
[31m+   will-change: transform, opacity;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+ `;[39m
[31m+ let heroWordRevealStyleInjected = false;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function RevealWords({ text }: { text: string }) {[39m
[31m+   const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();[39m
[31m+   // Moved off Framer Motion's JS-driven spring and onto a plain CSS[39m
[31m+   // @keyframes animation. The headline reveal fires right at page load,[39m
[31m+   // the exact moment the main thread is also busy with hydration, the[39m
[31m+   // radar sweep starting up, the marquee, and everything else initializing[39m
[31m+   // at once — a JS animation has to keep recalculating each frame on that[39m
[31m+   // same busy thread, while a CSS animation is handed off to the browser's[39m
[31m+   // compositor and keeps running smoothly even if the main thread is[39m
[31m+   // briefly congested. The stagger/timing/easing feel is unchanged.[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (heroWordRevealStyleInjected || typeof document === "undefined") return;[39m
[31m+     heroWordRevealStyleInjected = true;[39m
[31m+     const style = document.createElement("style");[39m
[31m+     style.textContent = HERO_WORD_REVEAL_CSS;[39m
[31m+     document.head.appendChild(style);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+   if (reduceMotion) return <>{text}</>;[39m
[31m+   const words = text.split(" ");[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <>[39m
[31m+       {words.map((word, i) => ([39m
[31m+         <span key={i} className="inline-block overflow-hidden align-top pb-[0.1em] -mb-[0.1em]">[39m
[31m+           <span className="hero-word-in" style={{ animationDelay: `${0.1 + i * 0.055}s` }}>[39m
[31m+             {word}[39m
[31m+             {i < words.length - 1 ? " " : ""}[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+         </span>[39m
[31m+       ))}[39m
[31m+     </>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function HeroCta({[39m
[31m+   href, magneticStrength, children, style, className, target, rel,[39m
[31m+ }: {[39m
[31m+   href: string; magneticStrength: number; children: ReactNode; style?: React.CSSProperties; className: string; target?: string; rel?: string;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   const magnet = useMagnetic<HTMLAnchorElement>(magneticStrength);[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <motion.a[39m
[31m+       ref={magnet.ref}[39m
[31m+       href={href}[39m
[31m+       target={target}[39m
[31m+       rel={rel}[39m
[31m+       onMouseMove={magnet.onMouseMove}[39m
[31m+       onMouseLeave={magnet.onMouseLeave}[39m
[31m+       onClick={() => {[39m
[31m+         // See src/app/three/scenePause.ts for why this exists.[39m
[31m+         if (href.startsWith("#")) pauseHeavyScenesForNav();[39m
[31m+       }}[39m
[31m+       style={{ ...style, x: magnet.style.x, y: magnet.style.y }}[39m
[31m+       whileHover={{ scale: 1.035 }}[39m
[31m+       whileTap={{ scale: 0.97 }}[39m
[31m+       transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 380, damping: 24 }}[39m
[31m+       className={className}[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       {children}[39m
[31m+     </motion.a>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const HERO_TRUSTINDEX_LOADER_SRC = "https://cdn.trustindex.io/loader.js?e7ec0f57824081211c46a9bf46f";[39m
[31m+ const HERO_TRUSTINDEX_REVIEW_LABEL = "3.5k+ reviews";[39m
[31m+ const HERO_TRUSTINDEX_RATING = "4.5";[39m
[31m+ let heroTrustindexScriptInjected = false;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const HeroTrustindexWidget = memo(function HeroTrustindexWidget() {[39m
[31m+   const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const container = containerRef.current;[39m
[31m+     if (!container || heroTrustindexScriptInjected) return;[39m
[31m+     heroTrustindexScriptInjected = true;[39m
[31m+     const script = document.createElement("script");[39m
[31m+     script.defer = true;[39m
[31m+     script.async = true;[39m
[31m+     script.src = HERO_TRUSTINDEX_LOADER_SRC;[39m
[31m+     container.appendChild(script);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const container = containerRef.current;[39m
[31m+     if (!container) return;[39m
[31m+     // Was setInterval polling every 200ms for up to 8s — that's real,[39m
[31m+     // recurring main-thread work landing in the exact window where the[39m
[31m+     // headline is also animating in. MutationObserver is event-driven: it[39m
[31m+     // costs nothing until the third-party script actually changes the DOM,[39m
[31m+     // instead of checking on a timer whether anything happened yet.[39m
[31m+     let done = false;[39m
[31m+     const tryPatch = () => {[39m
[31m+       if (done) return;[39m
[31m+       let patchedReviews = false;[39m
[31m+       const strongs = container.querySelectorAll(".ti-widget strong");[39m
[31m+       for (const el of strongs) {[39m
[31m+         const text = el.textContent || "";[39m
[31m+         if (/reviews/i.test(text)) {[39m
[31m+           const node = el as HTMLElement;[39m
[31m+           node.textContent = HERO_TRUSTINDEX_REVIEW_LABEL;[39m
[31m+           node.style.fontSize = "14px";[39m
[31m+           node.style.fontWeight = "600";[39m
[31m+           node.style.letterSpacing = "0.01em";[39m
[31m+           patchedReviews = true;[39m
[31m+         } else if (/^\d\.\d$/.test(text.trim())) {[39m
[31m+           (el as HTMLElement).textContent = HERO_TRUSTINDEX_RATING;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       if (patchedReviews) {[39m
[31m+         done = true;[39m
[31m+         observer.disconnect();[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     const observer = new MutationObserver(tryPatch);[39m
[31m+     observer.observe(container, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true });[39m
[31m+     tryPatch();[39m
[31m+     return () => observer.disconnect();[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return <div ref={containerRef} className="max-w-full overflow-x-hidden min-h-[32px] lg:min-h-[36px]" />;[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const HERO_TRUSTINDEX_MOBILE_LOADER_SRC = "https://cdn.trustindex.io/loader.js?789d2d278999828b52568075d8b";[39m
[31m+ let heroTrustindexMobileScriptInjected = false;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const HeroTrustindexMobileWidget = memo(function HeroTrustindexMobileWidget() {[39m
[31m+   const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const container = containerRef.current;[39m
[31m+     if (!container || heroTrustindexMobileScriptInjected) return;[39m
[31m+     heroTrustindexMobileScriptInjected = true;[39m
[31m+     const script = document.createElement("script");[39m
[31m+     script.defer = true;[39m
[31m+     script.async = true;[39m
[31m+     script.src = HERO_TRUSTINDEX_MOBILE_LOADER_SRC;[39m
[31m+     container.appendChild(script);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const container = containerRef.current;[39m
[31m+     if (!container) return;[39m
[31m+     // Same fix as the desktop widget above — MutationObserver instead of a[39m
[31m+     // 200ms setInterval poll running during the busy hero-load window.[39m
[31m+     let done = false;[39m
[31m+     const tryPatch = () => {[39m
[31m+       if (done) return;[39m
[31m+       const widget = container.querySelector(".ti-widget");[39m
[31m+       const strong = widget?.querySelector(".ti-fade-container strong");[39m
[31m+       if (widget && strong && !widget.querySelector(".fyt-ti-review-count")) {[39m
[31m+         done = true;[39m
[31m+         observer.disconnect();[39m
[31m+         if (/^\d\.\d$/.test((strong.textContent || "").trim())) {[39m
[31m+           strong.textContent = HERO_TRUSTINDEX_RATING;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         const span = document.createElement("span");[39m
[31m+         span.className = "fyt-ti-review-count";[39m
[31m+         span.style.fontSize = "13px";[39m
[31m+         span.style.fontWeight = "600";[39m
[31m+         span.style.color = "#000000";[39m
[31m+         span.style.marginLeft = "4px";[39m
[31m+         span.style.letterSpacing = "0.01em";[39m
[31m+         span.style.whiteSpace = "nowrap";[39m
[31m+         span.textContent = `| ${HERO_TRUSTINDEX_REVIEW_LABEL}`;[39m
[31m+         strong.after(span);[39m
[31m+         const style = document.createElement("style");[39m
[31m+         style.textContent = `.ti-widget[data-pid="789d2d278999828b52568075d8b"] .ti-header strong{font-size:13px;font-weight:600}.ti-widget[data-pid="789d2d278999828b52568075d8b"] .ti-header{padding:8px 10px}.ti-widget[data-pid="789d2d278999828b52568075d8b"] .fyt-ti-review-count{font-size:13px;font-weight:600}`;[39m
[31m+         widget.prepend(style);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     const observer = new MutationObserver(tryPatch);[39m
[31m+     observer.observe(container, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true });[39m
[31m+     tryPatch();[39m
[31m+     return () => observer.disconnect();[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return <div ref={containerRef} className="max-w-full overflow-x-hidden min-h-[26px]" />;[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function HeroTrustindexGate() {[39m
[31m+   const [isDesktop, setIsDesktop] = useState(() => window.matchMedia("(min-width: 1024px)").matches);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const query = window.matchMedia("(min-width: 1024px)");[39m
[31m+     const update = () => setIsDesktop(query.matches);[39m
[31m+     update();[39m
[31m+     query.addEventListener("change", update);[39m
[31m+     return () => query.removeEventListener("change", update);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Mobile: swapped with the award badge (which now sits here at the top,[39m
[31m+   // while the mobile Trustindex widget moved down to AwardBadgeMobileBar's[39m
[31m+   // old spot, below the hero) — desktop is unchanged.[39m
[31m+   return isDesktop ? <HeroTrustindexWidget /> : <AwardBadge />;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const HERO_ANIMATIONS_ACTIVE_MARGIN_PX = 600;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function Hero() {[39m

[31m+     const el = heroRef.current;[39m
[31m+     if (!el || typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") return;[39m
[31m+     const observer = new IntersectionObserver([39m
[31m+       (entries) => setAnimationsActive(!!entries[0]?.isIntersecting),[39m
[31m+       { rootMargin: `${HERO_ANIMATIONS_ACTIVE_MARGIN_PX}px 0px ${HERO_ANIMATIONS_ACTIVE_MARGIN_PX}px 0px` },[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     observer.observe(el);[39m
[31m+     return () => observer.disconnect();[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div[39m
[31m+       ref={heroRef}[39m
[31m+       className={`bg-[#03060d] relative flex flex-col items-center overflow-hidden shrink-0 w-full min-h-[620px] lg:h-[1080px] pt-[28px] pb-[64px] lg:py-0 ${animationsActive ? "" : "hero-anims-paused"}`}[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       <HeroBackground />[39m
[31m+       {/* Content — centered; on desktop pinned into the upper band per spec (top 130, h 496) */}[39m

[31m+             initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}[39m
[31m+             animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}[39m
[31m+             transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}[39m

[31m+         <div className="h-[20px] lg:h-[24px] shrink-0" />[39m
[31m+         <h1[39m
[31m+           className="font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-medium text-[44px] lg:text-[80px] w-full lg:max-w-[1264px]"[39m
[31m+           style={{ lineHeight: "104%", letterSpacing: "-0.025em", textShadow: "0px 2px 36px rgba(0,0,0,0.45)" }}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           {/* Design spec capitalizes "Without" — HERO_CONTENT.headlineMain (live-site manifest, lowercase "without") is correct as a record of the live site but not used for this specific render */}[39m
[31m+           <span className="block text-white"><RevealWords text="Trade Without Hidden Rules." /></span>[39m
[31m+           <span className="block text-[#60a5fa]" style={{ textShadow: "0 0 28px rgba(96,165,250,0.08)" }}><RevealWords text={HERO_CONTENT.headlineBlue} /></span>[39m

[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * Fixed header + a spacer that reserves its full (expanded) height in the page.[39m
[31m+  * Because the header is fixed rather than sticky, it can fold down on phones[39m
[31m+  * while scrolling without shifting any content below it.[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ function SiteHeader({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {[39m
[31m+   const mode = useMobileHeaderMode();[39m
[31m+   const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);[39m
[31m+   const compactRef = useRef(mode.compact);[39m
[31m+   compactRef.current = mode.compact;[39m
[31m+   const [spacer, setSpacer] = useState(0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useLayoutEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const el = ref.current;[39m
[31m+     if (!el) return;[39m
[31m+     const measure = () => {[39m
[31m+       if (!compactRef.current) setSpacer(el.offsetHeight);[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     measure();[39m
[31m+     if (typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined") return;[39m
[31m+     const ro = new ResizeObserver(measure);[39m
[31m+     ro.observe(el);[39m
[31m+     return () => ro.disconnect();[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <HeaderModeContext.Provider value={mode}>[39m
[31m+       <div ref={ref} className="fixed inset-x-0 top-0 z-40 w-full">[39m
[31m+         {children}[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div aria-hidden="true" className="w-full shrink-0" style={{ height: spacer }} />[39m
[31m+     </HeaderModeContext.Provider>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ function useHeroStageScale() {[39m
[31m+   const [scale, setScale] = useState(() => heroStageScale(window.innerWidth));[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const onResize = () => setScale(heroStageScale(window.innerWidth));[39m
[31m+     window.addEventListener("resize", onResize);[39m
[31m+     return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", onResize);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+   return scale;[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ function MobileMenu({ open, onClose }: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void }) {[39m
[31m+   const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();[39m
[31m+   const [affiliateOpen, setAffiliateOpen] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!open) setAffiliateOpen(false);[39m
[31m+   }, [open]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <AnimatePresence>[39m
[31m+       {open && ([39m
[31m+         <motion.div[39m
[31m+           initial={{ opacity: 0 }}[39m
[31m+           animate={{ opacity: 1 }}[39m
[31m+           exit={{ opacity: 0 }}[39m
[31m+           transition={{ duration: reduceMotion ? 0 : 0.3 }}[39m
[31m+           className="lg:hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-[rgba(10,14,24,0.9)] flex flex-col justify-between px-[24px]"[39m
[31m+           style={{[39m
[31m+             backdropFilter: "blur(28px) saturate(180%)",[39m
[31m+             WebkitBackdropFilter: "blur(28px) saturate(180%)",[39m
[31m+             paddingTop: "calc(32px + var(--safe-top))",[39m
[31m+             paddingBottom: "calc(40px + var(--safe-bottom))",[39m
[31m+           }}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <motion.div[39m
[31m+             initial={reduceMotion ? false : { y: -24, opacity: 0 }}[39m
[31m+             animate={{ y: 0, opacity: 1 }}[39m
[31m+             exit={reduceMotion ? undefined : { y: -16, opacity: 0 }}[39m
[31m+             transition={{ duration: reduceMotion ? 0 : 0.3, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}[39m
[31m+             className="flex flex-col justify-between h-full"[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+           {/* Header row */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex items-center justify-between">[39m
[31m+             <Wordmark />[39m
[31m+             <button[39m
[31m+               onClick={onClose}[39m
[31m+               aria-label="Close menu"[39m
[31m+               className="flex items-center justify-center size-[44px] rounded-full border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.25)] bg-transparent cursor-pointer transition-colors duration-200 hover:bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.08)]"[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none">[39m
[31m+                 <path d="M12 4L4 12M4 4L12 12" stroke="white" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeLinecap="round" />[39m
[31m+               </svg>[39m
[31m+             </button>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           {/* Centered links */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex flex-col items-center w-full gap-[2px] overflow-y-auto">[39m
[31m+             {NAV_LINKS.items.map((item) =>[39m
[31m+               item.type === "dropdown" ? ([39m
[31m+                 <div key={item.label} className="flex flex-col items-center w-full">[39m
[31m+                   <button[39m
[31m+                     type="button"[39m
[31m+                     aria-expanded={affiliateOpen}[39m
[31m+                     aria-controls="mobile-affiliate-submenu"[39m
[31m+                     onClick={() => setAffiliateOpen((v) => !v)}[39m
[31m+                     className={`${MOBILE_NAV_ITEM_CLASS} flex items-center justify-center gap-[8px] bg-transparent border-0 cursor-pointer`}[39m
[31m+                   >[39m
[31m+                     <span>{item.label}</span>[39m
[31m+                     <svg[39m
[31m+                       width="12"[39m
[31m+                       height="12"[39m
[31m+                       viewBox="0 0 12 12"[39m
[31m+                       fill="none"[39m
[31m+                       aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+                       className="transition-transform duration-200"[39m
[31m+                       style={{ transform: affiliateOpen ? "rotate(180deg)" : "rotate(0deg)" }}[39m
[31m+                     >[39m
[31m+                       <path d="M2.5 4.5L6 8l3.5-3.5" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" />[39m
[31m+                     </svg>[39m
[31m+                   </button>[39m
[31m+                   {affiliateOpen && ([39m
[31m+                     <div id="mobile-affiliate-submenu" className="flex flex-col items-center w-full pb-[4px]">[39m
[31m+                       {item.items.map((sub) => ([39m
[31m+                         <a key={sub.label} href={sub.href} className={MOBILE_NAV_SUBITEM_CLASS}>[39m
[31m+                           {sub.label}[39m
[31m+                         </a>[39m
[31m+                       ))}[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               ) : ([39m
[31m+                 <a key={item.label} href={item.href} className={MOBILE_NAV_ITEM_CLASS}>[39m
[31m+                   {item.label}[39m
[31m+                 </a>[39m
[31m+               ),[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           {/* Bottom actions */}[39m
[31m+           <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-[20px]">[39m
[31m+             <a href={NAV_LINKS.login.href} className="text-[17px] leading-[1.3] tracking-normal text-white font-['Inter:Medium',sans-serif] font-medium no-underline transition-colors duration-200 active:text-[#93c5fd]">{NAV_LINKS.login.label}</a>[39m
[31m+             <a href={NAV_LINKS.buyHere.href} onClick={onClose} className="bg-[#3b82f6] w-full rounded-[8px] py-[16px] flex items-center justify-center no-underline transition-transform duration-200 active:scale-[0.98]">[39m
[31m+               <p className="font-['Inter:Semi_Bold',sans-serif] font-semibold text-[16px] text-white">{NAV_LINKS.buyHere.label}</p>[39m
[31m+             </a>[39m
[31m+             <div className="h-[4px] w-[134px] rounded-full bg-[rgba(255,255,255,0.3)]" />[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           </motion.div>[39m
[31m+         </motion.div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </AnimatePresence>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** Phone header mode (compact / nav tucked away), shared by the banner and the nav. */[39m
[31m+ const HeaderModeContext = createContext<MobileHeaderMode>({ compact: false, navHidden: false });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function Nav() {[39m
[31m+   const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const scrolled = useScrollShrink(40);[39m
[31m+   const { compact, navHidden } = useContext(HeaderModeContext);[39m
[31m+   const tucked = navHidden && !menuOpen;[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="w-full">[39m
[31m+       <div>[39m
[31m+         <div[39m
[31m+           className="fyt-nav-glass relative z-[1] border-b rounded-b-[22px] transition-[background-color,backdrop-filter,box-shadow,transform,opacity] duration-300 ease-out"[39m
[31m+           style={{[39m
[31m+             transform: tucked ? "translate3d(0,-100%,0)" : "translate3d(0,0,0)",[39m
[31m+             opacity: tucked ? 0 : 1,[39m
[31m+             pointerEvents: tucked ? "none" : undefined,[39m
[31m+             background: scrolled ? "rgba(10,14,24,0.85)" : "rgba(10,14,24,0.35)",[39m
[31m+             backdropFilter: scrolled ? "blur(16px) saturate(180%)" : "blur(12px) saturate(180%)",[39m
[31m+             WebkitBackdropFilter: scrolled ? "blur(16px) saturate(180%)" : "blur(12px) saturate(180%)",[39m
[31m+             borderColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)",[39m
[31m+             boxShadow: scrolled ? "0 16px 48px rgba(0,0,0,0.45)" : "0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",[39m
[31m+           }}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           {/* The header is position:fixed with a spacer (see SiteHeader), so on[39m
[31m+               phones the bar can slim down after scrolling without reflowing[39m
[31m+               the page below it. Desktop keeps the full height. */}[39m
[31m+           <div className={`flex flex-row items-center rounded-[inherit] size-full transition-[height] duration-300 ease-out ${compact ? "h-[54px]" : "h-[72px]"} lg:h-[76px]`}>[39m
[31m+             <div className="content-stretch grid grid-cols-3 items-center px-[20px] lg:px-[26px] relative size-full max-w-[1280px] mx-auto">[39m
[31m+               <div className={`col-start-1 justify-self-start origin-left transition-transform duration-300 ease-out ${compact ? "scale-[0.84]" : ""} lg:scale-100`}><Brand /></div>[39m
[31m+               <div className="col-start-2 justify-self-center"><Links /></div>[39m
[31m+               <div className="col-start-3 justify-self-end flex items-center">[39m
[31m+                 {/* Desktop right cluster */}[39m
[31m+                 <div className="hidden lg:flex"><NavRight /></div>[39m
[31m+                 {/* Mobile: hamburger only — Log in + Start evaluation live in the overlay */}[39m
[31m+                 <div className={`origin-right transition-transform duration-300 ease-out ${compact ? "scale-[0.84]" : ""}`}>[39m
[31m+                   <HamburgerButton onClick={() => setMenuOpen(true)} />[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const PROMO_BANNER_ACTIVE_MARGIN_PX = 600;[39m
[31m+ /** "40% off + Buy 1 Get 3" for the one-line phone banner (drops "Limited Time:" and "Instantly"). */[39m
[31m+ const PROMO_DEAL_SHORT = PROMO_DEAL_LINE.replace(/^limited time:\s*/i, "").replace(/\s*instantly\s*$/i, "");[39m
[31m+ const PROMO_BANNER_GRADIENT =[39m
[31m+   "linear-gradient(90deg, #172554 0%, #1d4ed8 28%, #2563eb 50%, #1d4ed8 72%, #172554 100%)";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function copyTextToClipboard(text: string): Promise<boolean> {[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     if (navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {[39m
[31m+       await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);[39m
[31m+       return true;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   } catch {[39m
[31m+     // Fall through to the legacy path below.[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const input = document.createElement("textarea");[39m
[31m+     input.value = text;[39m
[31m+     input.setAttribute("readonly", "");[39m
[31m+     input.style.position = "fixed";[39m
[31m+     input.style.left = "-9999px";[39m
[31m+     input.style.top = "0";[39m
[31m+     document.body.appendChild(input);[39m
[31m+     input.focus();[39m
[31m+     input.select();[39m
[31m+     const ok = document.execCommand("copy");[39m
[31m+     document.body.removeChild(input);[39m
[31m+     return ok;[39m
[31m+   } catch {[39m
[31m+     return false;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function PromoCountdownPill({ compact = false, pulse }: { compact?: boolean; pulse: boolean }) {[39m
[31m+   const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now());[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const id = window.setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);[39m
[31m+     return () => window.clearInterval(id);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const left = formatHoursLeft(PROMO_DEADLINE, now);[39m
[31m+   if (left.expired) return null;[39m
[31m+   const totalHours = Number(left.hh);[39m
[31m+   const days = Math.floor(totalHours / 24);[39m
[31m+   const hh = String(totalHours % 24).padStart(2, "0");[39m
[31m+   const label = compact[39m
[31m+     ? `${days > 0 ? `${days}D ` : ""}${hh}:${left.mm}:${left.ss} Left`[39m
[31m+     : `${days > 0 ? `${days}D ` : ""}${hh}H ${left.mm}M ${left.ss}S Left`;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <span[39m
[31m+       role="timer"[39m
[31m+       aria-label={`${days > 0 ? `${days} days ` : ""}${Number(hh)} hours ${Number(left.mm)} minutes left`}[39m
[31m+       className={`inline-flex shrink-0 items-center rounded-full font-['Inter:Bold',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none tracking-[0.04em] tabular-nums text-white ${compact ? "h-[16px] px-[7px] text-[7.5px]" : "h-[24px] px-[12px] text-[11px] lg:h-[25px] lg:text-[12px]"}`}[39m
[31m+       style={{[39m
[31m+         background: "#DC2626",[39m
[31m+         border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.28)",[39m
[31m+         boxShadow: "0 2px 8px rgba(220,38,38,0.35), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.2)",[39m
[31m+         animation: pulse ? "fyt-promo-urgency-pulse 2.4s ease-in-out infinite" : undefined,[39m
[31m+       }}[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       {label}[39m
[31m+     </span>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function PromoBanner() {[39m
[31m+   const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();[39m
[31m+   const { compact } = useContext(HeaderModeContext);[39m
[31m+   const bannerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);[39m
[31m+   const [nearViewport, setNearViewport] = useState(true);[39m
[31m+   const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const copiedTimerRef = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const el = bannerRef.current;[39m
[31m+     if (!el || typeof IntersectionObserver === "undefined") return;[39m
[31m+     const observer = new IntersectionObserver([39m
[31m+       (entries) => setNearViewport(!!entries[0]?.isIntersecting),[39m
[31m+       { rootMargin: `${PROMO_BANNER_ACTIVE_MARGIN_PX}px 0px ${PROMO_BANNER_ACTIVE_MARGIN_PX}px 0px` },[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     observer.observe(el);[39m
[31m+     return () => observer.disconnect();[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => () => {[39m
[31m+     if (copiedTimerRef.current !== undefined) window.clearTimeout(copiedTimerRef.current);[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   function scrollToPricing() {[39m
[31m+     window.location.hash = "challenge";[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   function stopBannerNav(e: MouseEvent) {[39m
[31m+     e.preventDefault();[39m
[31m+     e.stopPropagation();[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   async function copyCode(e: MouseEvent) {[39m
[31m+     stopBannerNav(e);[39m
[31m+     const ok = await copyTextToClipboard(PROMO_CODE);[39m
[31m+     if (!ok) return;[39m
[31m+     setCopied(true);[39m
[31m+     if (copiedTimerRef.current !== undefined) window.clearTimeout(copiedTimerRef.current);[39m
[31m+     copiedTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1600);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   function CodePill({ compact = false }: { compact?: boolean }) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <button[39m
[31m+         type="button"[39m
[31m+         onMouseDown={stopBannerNav}[39m
[31m+         onClick={copyCode}[39m
[31m+         className={`inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-[5px] rounded-full border-0 px-[10px] cursor-pointer transition-transform duration-200 hover:scale-[1.04] active:scale-[0.97] ${compact ? "h-[18px]" : "h-[22px]"}`}[39m
[31m+         style={{ background: copied ? "#14532d" : "#0B1220", boxShadow: "0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.28), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.12)" }}[39m
[31m+         aria-label={copied ? `Copied ${PROMO_CODE}` : `Copy promo code ${PROMO_CODE}`}[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <span className={`font-['Inter:Bold',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none tracking-[0.06em] text-[#93c5fd] ${compact ? "text-[8px]" : "text-[9px]"}`}>[39m
[31m+           {copied ? "Copied" : "Code"}[39m
[31m+         </span>[39m
[31m+         <span className={`font-['Inter:Bold',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none tracking-[0.04em] text-white ${compact ? "text-[9px]" : "text-[10px]"}`}>{PROMO_CODE}</span>[39m
[31m+       </button>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div[39m
[31m+       ref={bannerRef}[39m
[31m+       role="link"[39m
[31m+       tabIndex={0}[39m
[31m+       onClick={scrollToPricing}[39m
[31m+       onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") { e.preventDefault(); scrollToPricing(); } }}[39m
[31m+       aria-label="View current promotions, jump to pricing"[39m
[31m+       className="group/promo relative z-[2] shrink-0 w-full cursor-pointer overflow-hidden"[39m
[31m+       style={{[39m
[31m+         background: PROMO_BANNER_GRADIENT,[39m
[31m+         boxShadow: "inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.22), inset 0 -1px 0 rgba(0,0,0,0.25), 0 8px 28px rgba(37,99,235,0.35)",[39m
[31m+         contain: "layout",[39m
[31m+       }}[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+         className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-px"[39m
[31m+         style={{ background: "linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, rgba(255,255,255,0.55), transparent)" }}[39m
[31m+       />[39m
[31m+       {!reduceMotion && ([39m
[31m+         <div[39m
[31m+           aria-hidden="true"[39m
[31m+           className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 w-1/3 opacity-40"[39m
[31m+           style={{[39m
[31m+             background: "linear-gradient(100deg, transparent 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0.18) 45%, rgba(255,255,255,0.28) 50%, rgba(255,255,255,0.18) 55%, transparent 100%)",[39m
[31m+             animation: nearViewport ? "fyt-promo-sheen 4.5s ease-in-out infinite" : undefined,[39m
[31m+           }}[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Mobile. At the top: 2 lines (deal + CODE, then benefits + countdown).[39m
[31m+           After scrolling: folds to one slim line (short deal + CODE + countdown). */}[39m
[31m+       <div className={`relative mx-auto flex w-full flex-col items-center justify-center px-[6px] transition-[padding] duration-300 ease-out md:hidden ${compact ? "py-[6px]" : "py-[7px]"}`}>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-x-[6px] whitespace-nowrap">[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none text-white whitespace-nowrap text-[10px] tracking-[0.01em] min-[380px]:text-[10.5px]">[39m
[31m+             {compact ? PROMO_DEAL_SHORT : PROMO_DEAL_LINE}[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+           <CodePill compact />[39m
[31m+           {compact && <PromoCountdownPill compact pulse={!reduceMotion && nearViewport} />}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div[39m
[31m+           className="grid w-full transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity] duration-300 ease-out"[39m
[31m+           style={{ gridTemplateRows: compact ? "0fr" : "1fr", opacity: compact ? 0 : 1 }}[39m
[31m+           aria-hidden={compact || undefined}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           <div className="overflow-hidden">[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-x-[5px] whitespace-nowrap pt-[5px]">[39m
[31m+               {PROMO_BENEFITS.map((text, i) => ([39m
[31m+                 <Fragment key={text}>[39m
[31m+                   {i > 0 && <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-[8px] w-px shrink-0 bg-white/35" />}[39m
[31m+                   <span className="font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none text-white whitespace-nowrap text-[8.5px] tracking-[0.01em] text-white/85">[39m
[31m+                     {text}[39m
[31m+                   </span>[39m
[31m+                 </Fragment>[39m
[31m+               ))}[39m
[31m+               {!compact && <PromoCountdownPill compact pulse={!reduceMotion && nearViewport} />}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Desktop / tablet: deal first, then benefits, then CODE + countdown.[39m
[31m+           Each group stays on one line, so a narrow screen wraps between groups, never mid-offer. */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="relative mx-auto hidden min-h-[36px] w-full max-w-[1440px] flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-x-[12px] gap-y-[6px] px-[12px] py-[8px] md:flex lg:gap-x-[14px] lg:px-[20px] xl:px-[24px]">[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-x-[10px] whitespace-nowrap lg:gap-x-[14px]">[39m
[31m+           <span className="font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none text-white whitespace-nowrap text-[12px] tracking-[0.02em] lg:text-[13px] xl:text-[14px]">[39m
[31m+             {PROMO_DEAL_LINE}[39m
[31m+           </span>[39m
[31m+           {PROMO_BENEFITS.map((text) => ([39m
[31m+             <Fragment key={text}>[39m
[31m+               <span aria-hidden="true" className="h-[12px] w-px shrink-0 bg-white/25" />[39m
[31m+               <span className="font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-bold uppercase leading-none text-white whitespace-nowrap text-[11px] tracking-[0.02em] text-white/85 lg:text-[12px] xl:text-[13px]">[39m
[31m+                 {text}[39m
[31m+               </span>[39m
[31m+             </Fragment>[39m
[31m+           ))}[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+         <div className="flex items-center gap-x-[8px] whitespace-nowrap lg:gap-x-[10px]">[39m
[31m+           <CodePill />[39m
[31m+           <PromoCountdownPill pulse={!reduceMotion && nearViewport} />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // The radar sweep's implementation was rebuilt below to be iOS-safe (single[39m
[31m+ // element, no blur filter, ~1/4 the layer area of the original — see the[39m
[31m+ // comment on the sweep-arm block). Flip to false again only if you need to[39m
[31m+ // rule it out during future debugging; the animation/timing logic itself[39m
[31m+ // (HERO_SWEEP_CSS, per-label glow keyframes) is unchanged from before.[39m
[31m+ const HERO_RADAR_ENABLED = true;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ function HeroStage({[39m
[31m+   labelScale = 1,[39m
[31m+   mobileLabels = false,[39m
[31m+   beamSuffix = "",[39m
[31m+   beamReachScale = 1,[39m
[31m+   sweepPaused = false,[39m
[31m+ }: {[39m
[31m+   labelScale?: number;[39m
[31m+   mobileLabels?: boolean;[39m
[31m+   beamSuffix?: string;[39m
[31m+   beamReachScale?: number;[39m
[31m+   sweepPaused?: boolean;[39m
[31m+ }) {[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <>[39m
[31m+       {/* Orbit rings — top halves of concentric ellipses */}[39m
[31m+       <svg className="absolute inset-0" width="1440" height="1080" viewBox="0 0 1440 1080" fill="none">[39m
[31m+         {HERO_ORBIT_RINGS.map((r, i) => ([39m
[31m+           <ellipse key={i} cx={r.cx} cy={r.cy} rx={r.rx} ry={r.ry} stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.07)" strokeWidth="1" />[39m
[31m+         ))}[39m
[31m+         {mobileLabels &&[39m
[31m+           MOBILE_ORBIT_LABELS.map(({ label, left, top }) => ([39m
[31m+             <line key={label} x1={720} y1={720} x2={left} y2={top + 5} stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.12)" strokeWidth="1" />[39m
[31m+           ))}[39m
[31m+       </svg>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Core glow behind the node */}[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         className="absolute"[39m
[31m+         style={{[39m
[31m+           width: 400, height: 340, left: 520, top: 550,[39m
[31m+           background: "radial-gradient(50% 50% at 50% 50%, rgba(115,166,255,0.32) 0%, rgba(59,130,245,0.13) 42%, rgba(10,36,92,0) 76%)",[39m
[31m+           filter: "blur(30px)",[39m
[31m+         }}[39m
[31m+       />[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {(mobileLabels ? MOBILE_ORBIT_LABELS : HERO_ORBIT_LABELS).map(({ label, left, top }, i) => {[39m
[31m+         const layout = mobileLabels ? MOBILE_LABEL_LAYOUT[i] : null;[39m
[31m+         const { items, textAlign, translateX, transformOrigin } = mobileLabelBoxCss(layout?.anchor);[39m
[31m+         const textSizeClass = layout ? "text-[26px] leading-[34px]" : "text-[22px] leading-[29px] whitespace-nowrap";[39m
[31m+         const glowClass = `hero-glow${beamSuffix}-${i}`;[39m
[31m+         return ([39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             key={label}[39m
[31m+             className={`absolute flex flex-col ${items} gap-[12px]`}[39m
[31m+             style={{[39m
[31m+               left,[39m
[31m+               top,[39m
[31m+               width: layout?.wrapWidth,[39m
[31m+               transform: `translateX(${translateX}) scale(${labelScale})`,[39m
[31m+               transformOrigin,[39m
[31m+             }}[39m
[31m+           >[39m
[31m+             <div className="rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ width: 10, height: 10, background: "#3B82F6", boxShadow: "0px 0px 8px rgba(59,130,246,0.8)" }} />[39m
[31m+             <p[39m
[31m+               className={`font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-medium ${textSizeClass} tracking-[-0.44px] text-white/90`}[39m
[31m+               style={{ textAlign }}[39m
[31m+             >[39m
[31m+               {label}[39m
[31m+             </p>[39m
[31m+             <div className={`${glowClass} absolute inset-0 flex flex-col ${items} gap-[12px]`} style={{ opacity: 0, animationPlayState: sweepPaused ? "paused" : "running", display: HERO_RADAR_ENABLED ? undefined : "none" }} aria-hidden>[39m
[31m+               <div className="rounded-full shrink-0" style={{ width: 10, height: 10, background: "#ffffff", boxShadow: "0px 0px 16px 4px rgba(255,255,255,0.9)" }} />[39m
[31m+               <p[39m
[31m+                 className={`font-['DM_Sans',sans-serif] font-medium ${textSizeClass} tracking-[-0.44px] text-white`}[39m
[31m+                 style={{ textShadow: "0px 0px 16px rgba(255,255,255,0.85)", textAlign }}[39m
[31m+               >[39m
[31m+                 {label}[39m
[31m+               </p>[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       })}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {HERO_RADAR_ENABLED && ([39m
[31m+         // Lightweight rebuild of the radar sweep. The original heavy[39m
[31m+         // version stacked three separately blurred (filter: blur(6px)) and[39m
[31m+         // masked panels inside an oversized 1720x1720px layer — that[39m
[31m+         // combination (large size + blur filter + mask-image + animation,[39m
[31m+         // times three) is a known iOS Safari GPU-crash pattern. A follow-up[39m
[31m+         // attempt using conic-gradient + mask-image avoided the crash but[39m
[31m+         // rendered with hard, aliased edges on-device instead of a soft[39m
[31m+         // fade — mask-image doesn't anti-alias reliably in combination with[39m
[31m+         // conic-gradient across browsers. This version drops mask-image[39m
[31m+         // entirely: a single radial-gradient, which has smooth built-in[39m
[31m+         // falloff on its own, shaped into a narrow cone that visually[39m
[31m+         // originates from the exact center pivot.[39m
[31m+         <div[39m
[31m+           className={`absolute hero-sweep-arm${beamSuffix}`}[39m
[31m+           style={{[39m
[31m+             width: 900 * beamReachScale, height: 900 * beamReachScale,[39m
[31m+             left: 720 - (450 * beamReachScale), top: 720 - (450 * beamReachScale),[39m
[31m+             transformOrigin: "50% 50%",[39m
[31m+             animationPlayState: sweepPaused ? "paused" : "running",[39m
[31m+           }}[39m
[31m+         >[39m
[31m+           {/* Rotate:0 baseline points DOWN — "Rewards on Demand" (rotate: 0[39m
[31m+               in heroOrbit.ts) sits below the node, not above it — so this[39m
[31m+               cone's tip sits at the wrapper's center (50%,50%) and it[39m
[31m+               extends toward the bottom, matching that baseline. */}[39m
[31m+           <div[39m
[31m+             className="absolute"[39m
[31m+             style={{[39m
[31m+               left: "50%", top: "50%",[39m
[31m+               width: 220 * beamReachScale, height: 400 * beamReachScale,[39m
[31m+               transform: "translateX(-50%)",[39m
[31m+               background:[39m
[31m+                 "radial-gradient(ellipse 50% 100% at 50% 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0.92) 0%, rgba(207,226,255,0.62) 16%, rgba(124,176,255,0.36) 40%, rgba(59,130,246,0.16) 65%, transparent 85%)",[39m
[31m+             }}[39m
[31m+           />[39m
[31m+         </div>[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* Node ring + core node with FYT logo (spec: 134px ring at 653,653; 96px node at 672,672) */}[39m
[31m+       <div className="absolute rounded-full border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.09)]" style={{ width: 134, height: 134, left: 653, top: 653 }} />[39m
[31m+       <div[39m
[31m+         className="absolute rounded-full border border-[rgba(255,255,255,0.15)] flex items-center justify-center"[39m
[31m+         style={{[39m
[31m+           width: 96, height: 96, left: 672, top: 672,[39m
[31m+           background: "linear-gradient(180deg, #1A1F2A 0%, #080A10 100%)",[39m
[31m+           boxShadow: "0px 0px 30px rgba(59,130,246,0.42), inset 0px 1px 0px rgba(255,255,255,0.13)",[39m
[31m+         }}[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <img alt="" src={imgImg10782} className="w-[70px] h-[66px] object-contain" />[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+ // Award badge — sits to the right of the trust strip on desktop[39m
[31m+ // (TrustStripDesktop, equal-width columns so both sides balance) and in[39m
[31m+ // its own bar directly below the hero on mobile (AwardBadgeMobileBar).[39m
[31m+ // Styled to match the trust pill's own understated chrome (subtle[39m
[31m+ // border + blur) rather than standing out with a glow.[39m
[31m+ function AwardBadge() {[39m
[31m+   // Image and text have separate behavior: hovering/tapping the badge[39m
[31m+   // IMAGE zooms it in place (magnifying-glass feel), never navigates.[39m
[31m+   // Tapping/clicking the TEXT navigates to the award page. `zoomed` is[39m
[31m+   // the tap-to-toggle state for touch devices, which have no hover;[39m
[31m+   // `whileHover` covers the mouse case on desktop on top of it.[39m
[31m+   const [zoomed, setZoomed] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+   const [hovered, setHovered] = useState(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Reset the zoom automatically on any page scroll (desktop hover state[39m
[31m+   // and mobile tap-toggle state alike), since a hover/tap can be left[39m
[31m+   // "stuck" zoomed in while the user then scrolls the page away.[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!zoomed && !hovered) return;[39m
[31m+     const handleScroll = () => {[39m
[31m+       setZoomed(false);[39m
[31m+       setHovered(false);[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     window.addEventListener("scroll", handleScroll, { passive: true });[39m
[31m+     return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", handleScroll);[39m
[31m+   }, [zoomed, hovered]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const isZoomed = zoomed || hovered;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <motion.div[39m
[31m+       initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 14 }}[39m
[31m+       whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}[39m
[31m+       viewport={{ once: true, margin: "-40px" }}[39m
[31m+       transition={{ duration: 0.55, delay: 0.15, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}[39m
[31m+       className="flex items-center gap-[16px] lg:gap-[18px] px-[18px] py-[12px] lg:px-[24px] lg:py-[15px] rounded-[16px] shrink-0 border backdrop-blur-[7px]"[39m
[31m+       style={{[39m
[31m+         background: "rgba(212,175,127,0.06)",[39m
[31m+         borderColor: "rgba(212,175,127,0.3)",[39m
[31m+       }}[39m
[31m+     >[39m
[31m+       <motion.button[39m
[31m+         type="button"[39m
[31m+         aria-label="Zoom the award badge image"[39m
[31m+         onClick={() => setZoomed((z) => !z)}[39m
[31m+         onMouseEnter={() => setHovered(true)}[39m
[31m+         onMouseLeave={() => setHovered(false)}[39m
[31m+         animate={{ scale: isZoomed ? 2.4 : 1 }}[39m
[31m+         transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 260, damping: 20 }}[39m
[31m+         className="relative z-10 shrink-0 bg-transparent border-0 p-0 cursor-zoom-in"[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <img[39m
[31m+           src={imgAwardBadge}[39m
[31m+           alt="BRM Brands Review Magazine - Fastest Growing Prop Trading Firm Global 2026"[39m
[31m+           className="w-[52px] h-[42px] lg:w-[62px] lg:h-[50px] object-contain pointer-events-none"[39m
[31m+           style={{ filter: "drop-shadow(0 6px 18px rgba(0,0,0,0.45))" }}[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+       </motion.button>[39m
[31m+       <a[39m
[31m+         href="https://brandsreviewmagazine.com/award-list-2026/"[39m
[31m+         target="_blank"[39m
[31m+         rel="noopener noreferrer"[39m
[31m+         className="flex flex-col gap-[3px] no-underline cursor-pointer text-left items-start"[39m
[31m+       >[39m
[31m+         <p className="font-['Inter:Semi_Bold',sans-serif] font-semibold text-[#d4af7f] text-[10px] lg:text-[11px] tracking-[1.5px] uppercase whitespace-nowrap">Officially Awarded</p>[39m
[31m+         <p className="font-['Inter:Bold',sans-serif] font-bold text-white text-[14px] lg:text-[16px] leading-[1.15] whitespace-nowrap">Fastest Growing Prop Trading Firm 2026</p>[39m
[31m+       </a>[39m
[31m+     </motion.div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // Mobile-only counterpart to the AwardBadge in TrustStripDesktop above.[39m
[31m+ // The hero's own mobile trust strip is absolutely positioned inside the[39m
[31m+ // hero graphic itself (TrustStripMobile), so this renders as an ordinary[39m
[31m+ // full-width dark bar right after the hero ends — reading as "just below"[39m
[31m+ // that whole section, trust strip included.[39m
[31m+ /** Mobile-only bar below the hero — now holds the Trustindex widget, swapped[39m
[31m+  * with the award badge which moved up to the top of the hero (see[39m
[31m+  * HeroTrustindexGate). Kept the original name to minimize the diff. */[39m
[31m+ function AwardBadgeMobileBar() {[39m
[31m+   return ([39m
[31m+     <div className="lg:hidden bg-[#050913] relative w-full flex items-center justify-center px-[20px] pt-[10px] pb-[20px]">[39m

[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
